## Notes

Ported: the local queue and the tail of the function. The original's body is
about 6,000 lines of Ghidra output, nearly all of it the network receive loop.

- The head (to about 0x4EE0A6): while the service provider is the null one
  (`g_pChitin + 0x96E`, `cNetwork.m_nServiceProvider`, read inline; ours calls
  `GetServiceProvider()`) or the game's `field_4BD4` (`g_pBaldurChitin + 0x1C54`
  is `m_pObjectGame`, `+ 0x4BD4`) is clear, the message list (`this + 0xC`,
  the CPtrList's count) is drained head first: `RemoveHead`, `Run()` (vtable
  0x18), the deleting destructor (vtable 0, flag 1). Ours is the same loop.
- The middle (to LAB_004f4975): while `cNetwork.m_bConnectionEstablished`
  (`g_pChitin + 0x1032`), `PeekMessage`/`FetchMessage`, then the switch on the
  message type and subtype that unmarshals other players' messages and calls
  the `CBaldurMessage::On...` handlers. **Omitted** per
  docs/adr/0002-single-player-only.md, with a NOTE in the source: single
  player never establishes a connection, so the loop does not run.
- The tail (from 0x4F4975): when hosting, `PurgeDroppedPlayers` and `ObjectControl`;
  then, when a dialog request is pending and its reply has returned
  (FUN_004f4f60, which reads `this + 0xD4`: ours is
  `CBaldurMessage::GetDialogReplyReturned`, whose address comment said
  0x4F4F50 and now says 0x4F4F60, see docs/evidence/4F4F60.md), the reply
  counter is decremented and the request cancelled when it drops below 0.
  Ported as is. `ObjectControl` (0x430C50) is still a stub in ours.

No oracle case: the queued messages act on the game's objects and areas,
which no fixture can honestly build. The world_entry replay compares the
outcome (the party placed in the start area).
